# Skills · C · part 227

812 entries, concept-check to concise-answers. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **concept-check**: Socratic quiz to test understanding of concepts from the learning journals. Generates targeted questions at three cognitive levels (recall, application, synthesis), evaluates answers, and flags gaps for spaced repetition. ([AutoQAce/optimizing_agents_contextual_engineering](https://github.com/AutoQAce/optimizing_agents_contextual_engineering/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/concept-check) · ★1)
- **concept-check**: Quiz the learner at the end of a study module (or on request) with 5-8 scenario-based questions about the module's concepts, one at a time, then grade with feedback and record weak spots. Use when the user says "quiz me", "concept check", "test my understanding", or finishes a module in docs/learning/roadmap.md. ([Tobote/claude-ai-base](https://github.com/Tobote/claude-ai-base/tree/HEAD/plugins/study-mode/skills/concept-check))
- **concept-clarifier**: 概念辨析工具（Concept Clarifier）。深度解析社会学及相关学科概念的理论谱系、 内部张力、边界条件与操作化方式。区分近似概念之间的实质差异，防止概念混用。 当用户说"这个概念是什么意思""XX和XX有什么区别""这个概念能用在我的研究里吗" "这个理论的核心概念是什么""概念怎么操作化""这两个概念可以混用吗" "帮我梳理这个概念"时触发此 skill。 注意：这不是百科全书式的定义查询工具，而是帮助研究者在研究中 精准使用概念、理解概念局限、避免贴标签的深度分析工具。 ([yipng05-max/-skills](https://github.com/yipng05-max/-skills/tree/HEAD/concept-clarifier) · ★333 · 2 copies)
- **concept-clarifier**: Resolve the open questions and hypothesis-marked glossary definitions produced by concept-extractor and bc-carver together with a human domain expert — via live interview or an async questionnaire (migration/INTERVIEW.md) — and fold confirmed answers into migration/GLOSSARY.md with provenance. Second step of a legacy-to-BCE migration; composes with concept-extractor. Invoke explicitly as /concept-clarifier in the analyzed project. Not for scoping a task or gathering requirements for a request — use clarify for that. ([AdamBien/airails](https://github.com/AdamBien/airails/tree/HEAD/migrations/concept-clarifier) · ★71 · 3 copies)
- **concept-clarity**: Build a precise, progressive mental model of a technical concept, code path, or invariant when an explanation or mechanism is difficult to follow. ([invertedmushroom/beat](https://github.com/invertedmushroom/beat/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/concept-clarity) · 2 copies)

Not for an unsolved DSA problem (dsa-tutor), a DSA statement to decode (problem-decoder), tracing a concrete input (dry-run-coach), an observed failure in real code (debug-coach), deriving complexity of real code (complexity-coach), designing… ([Far-200/think-before-code](https://github.com/Far-200/think-before-code/tree/HEAD/skills/concept-coach) · ★20)
- **concept-coach**: 产品经理的概念学习教练。当用户想了解一个新概念（任何领域：技术/商业/生活）时，用类比+白话讲清楚——类比先行、杀术语、短句、具体、不说教。与 thinking-coach 互补：thinking-coach 管"想"（追问式 sparring，教练不替思考），concept-coach 管"懂"（讲解式教学，直接讲明白）。支持自然语言扩展：比着讲（对比）、学习路径、引导（苏格拉底）、考考我（检验）、压短点（压缩）、做成网页（可视化）。全程可随时中止。 ([NEGU30/pmcraft](https://github.com/NEGU30/pmcraft/tree/HEAD/pmcraft-coach/skills/concept-coach))
- **concept-coalesce**: The mechanical half of concept capture. Reads the concept-stub notes, clusters the stubs that point at one idea, and — when ≥3 independent sourced signals coalesce AND the idea clears a "so what" gate — auto-aggregates them into a `concept` page (facts, no opinion). Never creates a hypothesis. Runs as a rem-cycle phase (weekly) or standalone. ([briney/scuderia](https://github.com/briney/scuderia/tree/HEAD/profiles/mnemo/skills/concept-coalesce) · ★1)
- **concept-code**: Build the runnable single-file PyTorch mini-project for a DL concept in src/learning/&lt;campaign&gt;/concepts/&lt;slug&gt;/ — either a small training run (MNIST-scale, minutes on the local GPU) or an analytical/math study (graphs and cost models, no gradients). Use after paper-distill produced notes.md with a mini-project sketch, or when the user asks for a minimal working example of a concept.
